00166: # -------------------- Themes / skins (v0.9.9) --------------------
00167: # Theme artwork ships with the plugin. The currently selected background is
00168: # copied to one stable /tmp path so every screen can reference the same pixmap.
00169: # Changing a theme therefore does not require rebuilding all Enigma2 skin XML.
00170: THEME_DIR = os.path.join(PLUGIN_DIR, "themes")
00171: THEME_CATALOG_FILE = os.path.join(THEME_DIR, "catalog.json")

00186: def _load_theme_catalog_early():
00187:     try:
00188:         with open(THEME_CATALOG_FILE, "r") as handle:
00189:             value = json.load(handle)
00190:         if isinstance(value, dict) and isinstance(value.get("themes"), dict):
00191:             return value
00192:     except Exception:
00193:         pass
00194:     return {
00195:         "groups": [{"id": "standard", "label": "Epi MediaHub", "themes": ["default"]}],
00196:         "themes": {"default": {"label": "Epi Dark", "group": "standard", "accent": "#1E88D8", "background": "default.png"}},
00197:     }
00198:

===== lines 341-456 =====
00341: def home_tile_widget(name, x, y, w=550, h=95, f=29):
00342:     # v0.9.7: tile glass is baked into HOME_GLASS_OVERLAY. Keeping the Label
00343:     # itself fully transparent is important on OpenATV: semi-transparent ARGB
00344:     # widget backgrounds can otherwise reveal the receiver skin/desktop rather
00345:     # than the EpiMediaHub ePixmap directly below it.
00346:     return '<widget name="%s" position="%s" size="%s" font="%s" halign="center" valign="center" foregroundColor="#F8FAFC" transparent="1" shadowColor="#000000" shadowOffset="2,2" zPosition="1" />' % (
00347:         name, pos(x, y), size(w, h), font(f)
00348:     )
00349: 
00350:

00352:     return '<ePixmap position="0,0" size="%d,%d" pixmap="%s" alphatest="blend" scale="1" zPosition="%d" />' % (GUI_W, GUI_H, path, z)

00370: 
00371: def list_widget(name, x, y, w, h, f=24, item_h=44):
00372:     # v0.9.7: the glass panel is an EpiMediaHub-owned PNG directly above the
00373:     # active theme background. The MenuList itself is transparent so Enigma2
00374:     # never composites a semi-transparent widget against the receiver/OpenATV
00375:     # desktop. This removes the last OpenATV bleed-through from categories,
00376:     # channel lists, VOD/series lists and search results.
00377:     return '<widget name="%s" position="%s" size="%s" font="%s" itemHeight="%d" scrollbarMode="showOnDemand" foregroundColor="#F4F7FB" backgroundColor="#00101418" transparent="1" selectionColor="#FFFFFF" selectionBackgroundColor="#00232D39" />' % (
00378:         name, pos(x, y), size(w, h), font(f), sy(item_h)
00379:     )

00728: 
00729: # -------------------- App language / localisation --------------------
00730: 
00731: _APP_LANGUAGE_CACHE = None
00732: SUPPORTED_APP_LANGUAGES = ("de", "en", "tr", "it", "es")
00733: 
00734: 
00735: def system_app_language():
00736:     try:
00737:         value = clean_text(enigma_language.getLanguage() if enigma_language is not None else "")
00738:     except Exception:
00739:         value = ""
00740:     value = value.lower().replace("-", "_")
00741:     prefix = value.split("_", 1)[0]
00742:     return prefix if prefix in SUPPORTED_APP_LANGUAGES else "de"
00743: 
00744: 
00745: def current_app_language():
00746:     global _APP_LANGUAGE_CACHE
00747:     if _APP_LANGUAGE_CACHE in SUPPORTED_APP_LANGUAGES:
00748:         return _APP_LANGUAGE_CACHE
00749:     settings = read_json(GLOBAL_SETTINGS_FILE, {})
00750:     selected = clean_text(settings.get("app_language", "system")).lower() if isinstance(settings, dict) else "system"
00751:     if selected == "system" or selected not in SUPPORTED_APP_LANGUAGES:
00752:         selected = system_app_language()
00753:     _APP_LANGUAGE_CACHE = selected
00754:     return selected

01789: def save_profiles(data):
01790:     return write_json(PROFILES_FILE, data)
01791: 
01792: 
01793: def get_profile(profile_id):
01794:     data = load_profiles()
01795:     for profile in data.get("profiles", []):
01796:         if profile.get("id") == profile_id:
01797:             return profile
01798:     return None
01799: 
01800: 
01801: def update_profile(profile):
01802:     data = load_profiles()
01803:     for index, existing in enumerate(data.get("profiles", [])):
01804:         if existing.get("id") == profile.get("id"):
01805:             data["profiles"][index] = profile
01806:             return save_profiles(data)
01807:     data["profiles"].append(profile)
01808:     return save_profiles(data)
01809: 
01810: 
01811: def set_active_profile(profile_id):
01812:     data = load_profiles()
01813:     if any(p.get("id") == profile_id for p in data.get("profiles", [])):
01814:         data["active_id"] = profile_id
01815:         return save_profiles(data)
01816:     return False

01818: 
01819: def attr_from_extinf(line, attr):
01820:     match = re.search(r'%s=["\']([^"\']*)["\']' % re.escape(attr), line, re.I)
01821:     return clean_text(match.group(1)) if match else ""
01822: 
01823: 
01824: def display_title_from_extinf(line):
01825:     if "," in line:

01830: def detect_epg_url(text):
01831:     for raw in text.splitlines()[:8]:
01832:         line = clean_text(raw)
01833:         if not line.upper().startswith("#EXTM3U"):
01834:             continue
01835:         for key in ("x-tvg-url", "url-tvg"):
01836:             value = attr_from_extinf(line, key)
01837:             if value:
01838:                 return value.split(",")[0].strip()
01839:     return ""

01841: 
01842: def classify_entry(title, group, url, tvg_id=""):
01843:     """Classify generic M3U entries conservatively.
01844: 
01845:     Explicit Xtream URL paths win. A real tvg-id is a strong live-TV signal and
01846:     is deliberately checked before fuzzy group-name heuristics. This prevents
01847:     live channels in groups such as "Film & Serien" from leaking into VOD.
01848:     """
01849:     haystack = (title + " " + group).lower()
01850:     path = url.lower().split("?", 1)[0]
01851:     if "/series/" in path:
01852:         return "series"
01853:     if "/movie/" in path:
01854:         return "movies"
01855:     if "/live/" in path:
01856:         return "live"
01857:     if clean_text(tvg_id):
01858:         return "live"
01859:     for pattern in (r"\bs\d{1,2}\s*e\d{1,3}\b", r"\b\d{1,2}x\d{1,3}\b", r"\bepisode\s*\d+\b", r"\bfolge\s*\d+\b"):
01860:         if re.search(pattern, haystack, re.I):
01861:             return "series"
01862:     if path.endswith((".mkv", ".mp4", ".avi", ".mov", ".m4v", ".wmv", ".iso")):
01863:         return "movies"
01864:     if any(word in haystack for word in ("series", "serien", "serie ", "tv shows", "tv-show", "episodes", "staffel", "season ", "boxset")):
01865:         return "series"
01866:     if any(word in haystack for word in ("movies", "movie ", "filme", "film ", "vod", "cinema", "kino", "peliculas", "película", "films")):
01867:         return "movies"
01868:     return "live"
01869: 
01870: 
01871: def _entry_from_extinf(line):
01872:     return {
01873:         "title": display_title_from_extinf(line),
01874:         "group": attr_from_extinf(line, "group-title") or "Ohne Kategorie",
01875:         "logo": attr_from_extinf(line, "tvg-logo"),
01876:         "tvg_id": attr_from_extinf(line, "tvg-id"),
01877:         "tvg_name": attr_from_extinf(line, "tvg-name"),
01878:     }
01879: 
01880: 
01881: def parse_m3u(text):
01882:     entries = []
01883:     pending = None
01884:     for raw in text.splitlines():
01885:         line = clean_text(raw)
01886:         if not line:
01887:             continue
01888:         if line.startswith("#EXTINF"):
01889:             pending = _entry_from_extinf(line)
01890:             continue
01891:         if line.startswith("#"):
01892:             continue
01893:         if pending is not None:
01894:             pending["url"] = line
01895:             pending["type"] = classify_entry(pending.get("title", ""), pending.get("group", ""), line, pending.get("tvg_id", ""))
01896:             entries.append(pending)
01897:             pending = None
01898:     return entries

02572: def _series_number(value, default=None):
02573:     """Return an integer season/episode number when providers use mixed formats."""
02574:     if value is None:
02575:         return default
02576:     if isinstance(value, bool):
02577:         return default
02578:     try:
02579:         return int(value)
02580:     except Exception:
02581:         pass
02582:     value = clean_text(value)
02583:     if not value:
02584:         return default
02585:     lowered = value.lower()
02586:     if "special" in lowered:
02587:         return 0
02588:     match = re.search(r"\d+", value)
02589:     if match:
02590:         try:
02591:             return int(match.group(0))
02592:         except Exception:
02593:             pass
02594:     return default

02600:         match = re.search(pattern, title)
02601:         if match:
02602:             try:
02603:                 return int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2))
02604:             except Exception:
02605:                 pass
02606:     return None, None

02650: def xtream_series_seasons(profile_id, entry):
02651:     """Load one series and return season records with only their own episodes."""
02652:     meta = load_xtream_meta(profile_id)
02653:     match = re.match(r"^xtream-series://(.+)$", clean_text(entry.get("url", "")))
02654:     if not match or meta.get("source") != "xtream":
02655:         return []
02656:     series_id = match.group(1)
02657:     data = _xtream_fetch(meta, "get_series_info", {"series_id": series_id}, timeout=30)
02658:     episodes = data.get("episodes", {}) if isinstance(data, dict) else {}
02659: 
02660:     grouped = {}
02661:     order_counter = 0
02662: 
02663:     def append_episode(raw_ep, season_hint=None):
02664:         nonlocal order_counter
02665:         normalised = _normalise_series_episode(meta, entry, series_id, raw_ep, season_hint, order_counter)
02666:         order_counter += 1
02667:         if not normalised:
02668:             return
02669:         season = normalised.get("season")
02670:         grouped.setdefault(season, []).append(normalised)
02671: 
02672:     if isinstance(episodes, dict):
02673:         # Xtream commonly returns {"1": [...], "2": [...]}. The key is the
02674:         # most reliable season number when individual episode rows omit it.
02675:         for season_key, values in episodes.items():
02676:             if isinstance(values, list):
02677:                 for ep in values:
02678:                     append_episode(ep, season_key)
02679:             elif isinstance(values, dict):
02680:                 append_episode(values, season_key)

03191: def expiry_summary(value):
03192:     if not value:
03193:         return "nicht verfügbar"
03194:     try:
03195:         expiry_time = time.mktime(time.strptime(value, "%d.%m.%Y"))
03196:         now = time.time()
03197:         days = int((expiry_time - now) / 86400.0)
03198:         if days < 0:
03199:             return "%s (abgelaufen)" % value
03200:         if days == 0:
03201:             return "%s (heute)" % value
03202:         return "%s (%d Tage)" % (value, days)
03203:     except Exception:
03204:         return value

03216: def audio_match_score(language, preferences):
03217:     value = clean_text(language).lower()
03218:     aliases = {
03219:         "deu": ("deu", "ger", "de", "deutsch", "german"),
03220:         "ita": ("ita", "it", "italiano", "italian"),
03221:         "tur": ("tur", "tr", "türkçe", "turkce", "turkish", "türkisch"),
03222:         "eng": ("eng", "en", "english"),
03223:     }
03224:     for rank, preferred in enumerate(preferences):
03225:         for alias in aliases.get(preferred, (preferred,)):
03226:             if value == alias or re.search(r"(^|[^a-z])%s([^a-z]|$)" % re.escape(alias), value):
03227:                 return rank
03228:     return 999

04696: def clean_media_title(title, kind):
04697:     text = clean_text(title)
04698:     text = re.sub(r"\[[^\]]+\]", " ", text)
04699:     text = re.sub(r"\([^)]*(?:4k|uhd|fhd|hd|multi|german|deutsch|ita|eng)[^)]*\)", " ", text, flags=re.I)
04700:     if kind == "series":
04701:         text = re.sub(r"\bS\d{1,2}\s*E\d{1,3}\b.*$", "", text, flags=re.I)
04702:         text = re.sub(r"\b\d{1,2}x\d{1,3}\b.*$", "", text, flags=re.I)
04703:     text = re.sub(r"\b(?:2160p|1080p|720p|4K|UHD|FHD|HD|HEVC|H265|H264|MULTI)\b", " ", text, flags=re.I)
04704:     text = re.sub(r"[_\.]+", " ", text)
04705:     text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip(" -|:")
04706:     return text

04714: def tmdb_request(path, params, settings):
04715:     key = clean_text(settings.get("tmdb_api_key", ""))
04716:     if not key:
04717:         raise Exception("Noch kein TMDb API-Key eingerichtet.")
04718:     params = dict(params or {})
04719:     headers = {"User-Agent": "EpiMediaHub/%s" % PLUGIN_VERSION, "Accept": "application/json"}
04720:     if key.startswith("eyJ"):
04721:         headers["Authorization"] = "Bearer %s" % key
04722:     else:
04723:         params["api_key"] = key
04724:     query = urlencode(params)
04725:     url = "https://api.themoviedb.org/3%s%s%s" % (path, "?" if query else "", query)
04726:     response = urlopen(Request(url, headers=headers), timeout=15)
04727:     raw = response.read()
04728:     response.close()
04729:     if isinstance(raw, bytes):
04730:         raw = raw.decode("utf-8", "ignore")
04731:     return json.loads(raw)

04735: def _saga_category_clean_name(value):
04736:     """Extract a franchise/search term from Saga/Collection category names."""
04737:     text = clean_text(value)
04738:     if not text or text == "__all__":
04739:         return ""
04740:     text = text.replace("_", " ")
04741:     # Typical provider variants: "Saga Harry Potter", "SAGA | Rocky",
04742:     # "[SAGA] Fast & Furious", "Collection - Mission Impossible".
04743:     text = re.sub(
04744:         r"^[\s\[\(\{]*(?:saga|collection|collections|kollektion|sammlung|franchise|filmreihe|reihe)[\s\]\)\}]*[:\-|>]*\s*",
04745:         "", text, flags=re.I
04746:     )
04747:     for _i in range(3):
04748:         text = re.sub(r"^\s*(?:DE|GER|GERMAN|EN|ENG|IT|ITA|TR|TUR|VIP|VOD|4K|UHD|FHD|HD|MOVIES?|FILME?)\s*[|:\->]+\s*", "", text, flags=re.I)
04749:         text = re.sub(r"\s*[|:\->]+\s*(?:DE|GER|GERMAN|EN|ENG|IT|ITA|TR|TUR|VIP|VOD|4K|UHD|FHD|HD|MOVIES?|FILME?)\s*$", "", text, flags=re.I)

04763:         raw,
04764:         flags=re.I

04798:     name = _saga_category_clean_name(value)
04799:     return hashlib.sha1(_saga_name_norm(name).encode("utf-8", "ignore")).hexdigest()

04802: SAGA_CACHE_VERSION = 1

04804: 
04805: def saga_category_cache_record(value):
04806:     if not _looks_like_saga_category(value):
04807:         return True, ""
04808:     cache = read_json(SAGA_CACHE_FILE, {})
04809:     if not isinstance(cache, dict):
04810:         return False, ""
04811:     record = cache.get(_saga_cache_key(value))
04812:     if not isinstance(record, dict):
04813:         return False, ""
04814:     if int(record.get("cache_version", 0) or 0) != SAGA_CACHE_VERSION:
04815:         return False, ""
04816:     age = int(time.time()) - int(record.get("cached_at", 0) or 0)
04817:     positive = bool(record.get("image_key") or record.get("image_url") or record.get("file_path"))
04818:     max_age = 45 * 86400 if positive else 2 * 86400
04819:     if age < 0 or age >= max_age:
04820:         return False, ""
04821:     image_key = clean_text(record.get("image_key", ""))
04822:     if not image_key:
04823:         seed = clean_text(record.get("file_path", "")) or clean_text(record.get("image_url", ""))
04824:         if seed:
04825:             image_key = hashlib.sha1(seed.encode("utf-8", "ignore")).hexdigest()
04826:     if not image_key:
04827:         return True, ""
04828:     return True, _cached_image_path(SAGA_CACHE_DIR, image_key)

04831: def _save_saga_cache_record(value, record):
04832:     cache = read_json(SAGA_CACHE_FILE, {})
04833:     if not isinstance(cache, dict):
04834:         cache = {}
04835:     record = dict(record or {})
04836:     record["cached_at"] = int(time.time())
04837:     record["cache_version"] = SAGA_CACHE_VERSION
04838:     cache[_saga_cache_key(value)] = record
04839:     write_json(SAGA_CACHE_FILE, cache)

04833:     if not isinstance(cache, dict):
04834:         cache = {}

04842: def _tmdb_saga_category_image(value, settings):
04843:     """Prefer TMDb collection artwork. A configured TMDb key enables this path."""
04844:     if not clean_text(settings.get("tmdb_api_key", "")):
04845:         return ""
04846:     query_name = _saga_category_clean_name(value)
04847:     if not query_name:
04848:         return ""
04849:     try:
04850:         result = tmdb_request(
04851:             "/search/collection",
04852:             {"query": query_name, "language": settings.get("metadata_language", "de-DE")},
04853:             settings
04854:         )
04855:         hits = result.get("results", []) if isinstance(result, dict) else []
04856:         ranked = []
04857:         for hit in hits:
04858:             if not isinstance(hit, dict):
04859:                 continue
04860:             title = clean_text(hit.get("name", "")) or clean_text(hit.get("title", ""))
04861:             score = _saga_name_match_score(query_name, title)
04862:             if hit.get("poster_path") or hit.get("backdrop_path"):
04863:                 score += 5
04864:             ranked.append((score, hit))
04865:         ranked.sort(key=lambda item: item[0], reverse=True)
04866:         hit = ranked[0][1] if ranked and ranked[0][0] >= 72 else None
04867:         if not isinstance(hit, dict):
04868:             return ""
04869: 
04870:         collection_id = hit.get("id")
04871:         # Some TMDb responses expose collection logos through the images
04872:         # endpoint. Prefer a transparent logo when available, then artwork.
04873:         file_path = ""
04874:         image_kind = ""
04875:         if collection_id:
04876:             try:
04877:                 images = tmdb_request("/collection/%s/images" % collection_id, {}, settings)
04878:                 logos = images.get("logos", []) if isinstance(images, dict) else []
04879:                 language = clean_text(settings.get("metadata_language", "de-DE")).split("-")[0].lower()
04880:                 preferred = []
04881:                 for logo in logos:
04882:                     if not isinstance(logo, dict) or not logo.get("file_path"):
04883:                         continue
04884:                     lang = clean_text(logo.get("iso_639_1", "")).lower()
04885:                     rank = 3 if lang == language else (2 if lang == "en" else (1 if not lang else 0))
04886:                     preferred.append((rank, float(logo.get("vote_average", 0) or 0), logo))
04887:                 preferred.sort(key=lambda item: (item[0], item[1]), reverse=True)
04888:                 if preferred:
04889:                     file_path = clean_text(preferred[0][2].get("file_path", ""))
04890:                     image_kind = "logo"
04891:             except Exception:
04892:                 pass
04893: 
04894:         if not file_path:
04895:             file_path = clean_text(hit.get("backdrop_path", "")) or clean_text(hit.get("poster_path", ""))
04896:             image_kind = "artwork"
04897:         if not file_path:
04898:             return ""
04899:         image_key = hashlib.sha1(file_path.encode("utf-8", "ignore")).hexdigest()
04900:         image_url = "https://image.tmdb.org/t/p/w500%s" % file_path
04901:         image_path = download_image_cached(image_url, SAGA_CACHE_DIR, key=image_key, timeout=12, max_bytes=5 * 1024 * 1024)
04902:         if image_path:
04903:             _save_saga_cache_record(value, {
04904:                 "query": query_name,
04905:                 "name": clean_text(hit.get("name", query_name)),
04906:                 "file_path": file_path,
04907:                 "image_url": image_url,
04908:                 "image_key": image_key,
04909:                 "tmdb_id": collection_id,
04910:                 "image_kind": image_kind,
04911:                 "source": "tmdb-collection",
04912:             })
04913:             return image_path
04914:     except Exception:
04915:         pass
04916:     return ""

04919: def _wikipedia_saga_category_image(value, settings):
04920:     """Key-free fallback for franchise/category artwork via Wikipedia thumbnails."""
04921:     query_name = _saga_category_clean_name(value)
04922:     wanted = _saga_name_norm(query_name)
04923:     if not wanted:
04924:         return ""
04925:     metadata_lang = clean_text(settings.get("metadata_language", "de-DE")).lower()
04926:     preferred = metadata_lang.split("-")[0] if metadata_lang else "de"
04927:     langs = []
04928:     for lang in (preferred, "de", "en"):
04929:         if lang in ("de", "en", "it", "tr") and lang not in langs:
04930:             langs.append(lang)
04931:     series_words = (
04932:         "filmreihe", "filmserie", "franchise", "film series", "media franchise",
04933:         "film franchise", "series of films", "films", "film saga", "saga"
04934:     )
04935:     headers = {"User-Agent": "EpiMediaHub/%s (saga-category-image)" % PLUGIN_VERSION, "Accept": "application/json"}
04936:     for lang in langs:
04937:         try:
04938:             params = {
04939:                 "action": "query",
04940:                 "generator": "search",
04941:                 "gsrsearch": "%s film series" % query_name,
04942:                 "gsrnamespace": "0",
04943:                 "gsrlimit": "8",
04944:                 "prop": "pageimages|pageterms",
04945:                 "piprop": "thumbnail",
04946:                 "pithumbsize": "500",
04947:                 "pilimit": "8",
04948:                 "wbptterms": "description",
04949:                 "format": "json",
04950:                 "formatversion": "2",
04951:                 "origin": "*",
04952:             }
04953:             url = "https://%s.wikipedia.org/w/api.php?%s" % (lang, urlencode(params))
04954:             response = urlopen(Request(url, headers=headers), timeout=10)
04955:             raw = response.read()
04956:             response.close()
04957:             if isinstance(raw, bytes):
04958:                 raw = raw.decode("utf-8", "ignore")
04959:             data = json.loads(raw)
04960:             pages = ((data.get("query") or {}).get("pages") or []) if isinstance(data, dict) else []
04961:             ranked = []
04962:             for page in pages:
04963:                 if not isinstance(page, dict):
04964:                     continue
04965:                 thumb = page.get("thumbnail", {}) if isinstance(page.get("thumbnail", {}), dict) else {}
04966:                 image_url = clean_text(thumb.get("source", ""))
04967:                 if not image_url:
04968:                     continue
04969:                 title = clean_text(page.get("title", ""))
04970:                 terms = page.get("terms", {}) if isinstance(page.get("terms", {}), dict) else {}
04971:                 descriptions = terms.get("description", [])
04972:                 if isinstance(descriptions, str):
04973:                     descriptions = [descriptions]
04974:                 desc = " ".join(clean_text(x) for x in descriptions).lower()
04975:                 score = _saga_name_match_score(query_name, title)
04976:                 if any(word in desc for word in series_words):
04977:                     score += 22
04978:                 ranked.append((score, title, image_url, desc))
04979:             ranked.sort(key=lambda item: item[0], reverse=True)
04980:             if ranked and ranked[0][0] >= 72:
04981:                 score, title, image_url, desc = ranked[0]
04982:                 image_key = hashlib.sha1(image_url.encode("utf-8", "ignore")).hexdigest()
04983:                 image_path = download_image_cached(image_url, SAGA_CACHE_DIR, key=image_key, timeout=12, max_bytes=5 * 1024 * 1024)
04984:                 if image_path:
04985:                     _save_saga_cache_record(value, {
04986:                         "query": query_name,
04987:                         "name": title,
04988:                         "image_url": image_url,
04989:                         "image_key": image_key,
04990:                         "source": "wikipedia-%s" % lang,
04991:                     })
04992:                     return image_path
04993:         except Exception:
04994:             continue
04995:     return ""
04996:

04999:     if not _looks_like_saga_category(value):
05000:         return ""
05001:     resolved, cached_path = saga_category_cache_record(value)
05002:     if resolved:
05003:         return cached_path
05004:     settings = load_global_settings()
05005:     path = _tmdb_saga_category_image(value, settings)
05006:     if path:
05007:         return path
05008:     path = _wikipedia_saga_category_image(value, settings)
05009:     if path:
05010:         return path
05011:     _save_saga_cache_record(value, {"query": _saga_category_clean_name(value), "source": "none"})
05012:     return ""

05014: def _actor_category_clean_name(value):
05015:     """Return a useful person-search term from a provider VOD category."""
05016:     text = clean_text(value)
05017:     if not text or text == "__all__":
05018:         return ""
05019:     text = text.replace("_", " ")
05020: 
05021:     # Providers use many variants such as "[SCHAUSPIELER] Jason Statham",
05022:     # "Filme mit Jason Statham" or "DE | Actor: Jason Statham".
05023:     text = re.sub(r"^[\s\[\(\{]*(?:schauspieler(?:in)?|schauspielerinnen|darsteller(?:in)?|darstellerinnen|actors?|actresses|cast|stars?)[\s\]\)\}]*[:\-|>]*\s*", "", text, flags=re.I)
05024:     text = re.sub(r"^\s*(?:filme?|movies?|serien|series|tv)[\s\-|:]+(?:mit|with|von|by)\s+", "", text, flags=re.I)
05025:     text = re.sub(r"^\s*(?:mit|with)\s+", "", text, flags=re.I)
05026:     text = re.sub(r"\s*[:\-|>]\s*(?:filme?|movies?|serien|series|tv|collection|kollektion)\s*$", "", text, flags=re.I)
05027: 
05028:     # Remove harmless quality/language/provider decorations around a name.
05029:     # Many IPTV providers use folders such as "VOD | MOVIES | Jason Statham"
05030:     # or "FILME - Jason Statham". Strip those generic wrappers repeatedly so
05031:     # TMDb receives the actual person's name instead of the whole folder title.
05032:     for _i in range(3):
05033:         text = re.sub(r"^\s*(?:DE|GER|GERMAN|EN|ENG|IT|ITA|TR|TUR|VIP|VOD|4K|UHD|FHD|HD)\s*[|:\->]+\s*", "", text, flags=re.I)
05034:         text = re.sub(r"^\s*(?:filme?|movies?|movie|serien|series|tv|kino|cinema|vod)\s*[|:\->]+\s*", "", text, flags=re.I)
05035:         text = re.sub(r"\s*[|:\->]+\s*(?:filme?|movies?|movie|serien|series|tv|kino|cinema|vod|4K|UHD|FHD|HD)\s*$", "", text, flags=re.I)
05036:     # A locale/provider prefix can expose the actor label only after the first
05037:     # cleanup pass, e.g. "DE | Actor: Jason Statham". Strip it once more.
05038:     text = re.sub(r"^[\s\[\(\{]*(?:schauspieler(?:in)?|schauspielerinnen|darsteller(?:in)?|darstellerinnen|actors?|actresses|cast|stars?)[\s\]\)\}]*[:\-|>]*\s*", "", text, flags=re.I)
05039:     text = re.sub(r"\s*[|:\-]\s*(?:DE|GER|GERMAN|EN|ENG|IT|ITA|TR|TUR|4K|UHD|FHD|HD|VOD)\s*$", "", text, flags=re.I)
05040:     text = re.sub(r"^\s*\d{1,3}\s*[.\-)]\s*", "", text)
05041:     text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip(" -|:[](){}")
05042:     return text
05043: 
05044: 
05045: def _actor_name_norm(value):
05046:     text = clean_text(value).lower()
05047:     # Parenthetical disambiguators from Wikipedia such as "(actor)" should not
05048:     # prevent an otherwise exact name match.
05049:     text = re.sub(r"\([^)]*\)", " ", text)
05050:     return re.sub(r"[^\w]+", " ", text, flags=re.UNICODE).strip()
05051: 
05052: 
05053: def _actor_explicit_label(value):

05021:     # Providers use many variants such as "[SCHAUSPIELER] Jason Statham",
05022:     # "Filme mit Jason Statham" or "DE | Actor: Jason Statham".

05028:     # Remove harmless quality/language/provider decorations around a name.
05029:     # Many IPTV providers use folders such as "VOD | MOVIES | Jason Statham"
05030:     # or "FILME - Jason Statham". Strip those generic wrappers repeatedly so
05031:     # TMDb receives the actual person's name instead of the whole folder title.
05032:     for _i in range(3):
05033:         text = re.sub(r"^\s*(?:DE|GER|GERMAN|EN|ENG|IT|ITA|TR|TUR|VIP|VOD|4K|UHD|FHD|HD)\s*[|:\->]+\s*", "", text, flags=re.I)
05034:         text = re.sub(r"^\s*(?:filme?|movies?|movie|serien|series|tv|kino|cinema|vod)\s*[|:\->]+\s*", "", text, flags=re.I)
05035:         text = re.sub(r"\s*[|:\->]+\s*(?:filme?|movies?|movie|serien|series|tv|kino|cinema|vod|4K|UHD|FHD|HD)\s*$", "", text, flags=re.I)
05036:     # A locale/provider prefix can expose the actor label only after the first
05037:     # cleanup pass, e.g. "DE | Actor: Jason Statham". Strip it once more.
05038:     text = re.sub(r"^[\s\[\(\{]*(?:schauspieler(?:in)?|schauspielerinnen|darsteller(?:in)?|darstellerinnen|actors?|actresses|cast|stars?)[\s\]\)\}]*[:\-|>]*\s*", "", text, flags=re.I)

05045: def _actor_name_norm(value):
05046:     text = clean_text(value).lower()
05047:     # Parenthetical disambiguators from Wikipedia such as "(actor)" should not
05048:     # prevent an otherwise exact name match.
05049:     text = re.sub(r"\([^)]*\)", " ", text)
05050:     return re.sub(r"[^\w]+", " ", text, flags=re.UNICODE).strip()

05102: def _actor_cache_key(value):
05103:     return hashlib.sha1(_actor_name_norm(_actor_category_clean_name(value)).encode("utf-8", "ignore")).hexdigest()
05104: 
05105: 
05106: ACTOR_CACHE_VERSION = 3
05107: 
05108: 
05109: def actor_category_cache_record(value):
05110:     if not _looks_like_actor_category(value):
05111:         return True, ""
05112:     cache = read_json(ACTOR_CACHE_FILE, {})
05113:     if not isinstance(cache, dict):
05114:         return False, ""
05115:     record = cache.get(_actor_cache_key(value))
05116:     if not isinstance(record, dict):
05117:         return False, ""
05118:     # Retry all v0.7.20 records once so old negative TMDb-only results do not
05119:     # suppress the new Wikipedia fallback.
05120:     if int(record.get("cache_version", 0) or 0) != ACTOR_CACHE_VERSION:
05121:         return False, ""
05122:     age = int(time.time()) - int(record.get("cached_at", 0) or 0)
05123:     positive = bool(record.get("image_key") or record.get("profile_path") or record.get("image_url"))
05124:     max_age = 30 * 86400 if positive else 2 * 86400
05125:     if age < 0 or age >= max_age:
05126:         return False, ""
05127:     image_key = clean_text(record.get("image_key", ""))
05128:     if not image_key:
05129:         seed = clean_text(record.get("profile_path", "")) or clean_text(record.get("image_url", ""))
05130:         if seed:
05131:             image_key = hashlib.sha1(seed.encode("utf-8", "ignore")).hexdigest()
05132:     if not image_key:
05133:         return True, ""
05134:     return True, _cached_image_path(ACTOR_CACHE_DIR, image_key)

05137: def _save_actor_cache_record(value, record):
05138:     cache = read_json(ACTOR_CACHE_FILE, {})
05139:     if not isinstance(cache, dict):
05140:         cache = {}
05141:     record = dict(record or {})

05148: def _actor_name_match_score(wanted, candidate):
05149:     wanted_norm = _actor_name_norm(wanted)
05150:     candidate_norm = _actor_name_norm(candidate)
05151:     if not wanted_norm or not candidate_norm:
05152:         return 0
05153:     if wanted_norm == candidate_norm:
05154:         return 100
05155:     w = set(wanted_norm.split())
05156:     c = set(candidate_norm.split())
05157:     if not w or not c:
05158:         return 0
05159:     common = len(w & c)
05160:     if common == len(w) and common >= 2:
05161:         return 85
05162:     if common >= 2:
05163:         return 60 + common
05164:     return 0

05175:             settings
05176:         )
05177:         hits = result.get("results", []) if isinstance(result, dict) else []

05148: def _actor_name_match_score(wanted, candidate):
05149:     wanted_norm = _actor_name_norm(wanted)
05150:     candidate_norm = _actor_name_norm(candidate)
05151:     if not wanted_norm or not candidate_norm:
05152:         return 0
05153:     if wanted_norm == candidate_norm:
05154:         return 100
05155:     w = set(wanted_norm.split())
05156:     c = set(candidate_norm.split())
05157:     if not w or not c:
05158:         return 0
05159:     common = len(w & c)
05160:     if common == len(w) and common >= 2:
05161:         return 85
05162:     if common >= 2:
05163:         return 60 + common
05164:     return 0

05167: def _tmdb_actor_category_image(value, settings):
05168:     if not clean_text(settings.get("tmdb_api_key", "")):
05169:         return ""
05170:     query_name = _actor_category_clean_name(value)
05171:     try:
05172:         result = tmdb_request(
05173:             "/search/person",
05174:             {"query": query_name, "language": settings.get("metadata_language", "de-DE"), "include_adult": "false"},
05175:             settings
05176:         )
05177:         hits = result.get("results", []) if isinstance(result, dict) else []
05178:         ranked = []
05179:         for hit in hits:
05180:             if not isinstance(hit, dict):
05181:                 continue
05182:             department = clean_text(hit.get("known_for_department", "")).lower()
05183:             score = _actor_name_match_score(query_name, hit.get("name", ""))
05184:             if department in ("acting", "schauspiel"):
05185:                 score += 15
05186:             if hit.get("profile_path"):
05187:                 score += 5
05188:             ranked.append((score, hit))
05189:         ranked.sort(key=lambda item: item[0], reverse=True)
05190:         hit = ranked[0][1] if ranked and ranked[0][0] >= (80 if not _actor_explicit_label(value) else 25) else None
05191:         if isinstance(hit, dict):
05192:             profile_path = clean_text(hit.get("profile_path", ""))
05193:             if profile_path:
05194:                 image_key = hashlib.sha1(profile_path.encode("utf-8", "ignore")).hexdigest()
05195:                 image_url = "https://image.tmdb.org/t/p/w185%s" % profile_path
05196:                 image_path = download_image_cached(image_url, ACTOR_CACHE_DIR, key=image_key, timeout=12, max_bytes=3 * 1024 * 1024)
05197:                 if image_path:
05198:                     _save_actor_cache_record(value, {
05199:                         "query": query_name,
05200:                         "profile_path": profile_path,
05201:                         "image_url": image_url,
05202:                         "image_key": image_key,
05203:                         "tmdb_id": hit.get("id"),
05204:                         "name": clean_text(hit.get("name", query_name)),
05205:                         "source": "tmdb",
05206:                     })
05207:                     return image_path
05208:     except Exception:
05209:         pass
05210:     return ""

05213: def _wikipedia_actor_category_image(value, settings):
05214:     """Key-free actor portrait fallback using Wikipedia page thumbnails."""
05215:     query_name = _actor_category_clean_name(value)
05216:     wanted = _actor_name_norm(query_name)
05217:     if not wanted:
05218:         return ""
05219:     metadata_lang = clean_text(settings.get("metadata_language", "de-DE")).lower()
05220:     preferred = metadata_lang.split("-")[0] if metadata_lang else "de"
05221:     langs = []
05222:     for lang in (preferred, "de", "en"):
05223:         if lang in ("de", "en", "it", "tr") and lang not in langs:
05224:             langs.append(lang)
05225:     actor_words = (
05226:         "actor", "actress", "schauspieler", "schauspielerin", "darsteller", "darstellerin",
05227:         "attore", "attrice", "oyuncu", "film actor", "television actor"
05228:     )
05229:     headers = {"User-Agent": "EpiMediaHub/%s (actor-category-image)" % PLUGIN_VERSION, "Accept": "application/json"}
05230:     for lang in langs:
05231:         try:
05232:             params = {
05233:                 "action": "query",
05234:                 "generator": "search",
05235:                 "gsrsearch": query_name,
05236:                 "gsrnamespace": "0",
05237:                 "gsrlimit": "6",
05238:                 "prop": "pageimages|pageterms",
05239:                 "piprop": "thumbnail",
05240:                 "pithumbsize": "240",
05241:                 "wbptterms": "description",
05242:                 "format": "json",
05243:                 "formatversion": "2",
05244:             }
05245:             url = "https://%s.wikipedia.org/w/api.php?%s" % (lang, urlencode(params))
05246:             response = urlopen(Request(url, headers=headers), timeout=12)
05247:             raw = response.read()
05248:             response.close()
05249:             if isinstance(raw, bytes):
05250:                 raw = raw.decode("utf-8", "ignore")
05251:             data = json.loads(raw)
05252:             pages = data.get("query", {}).get("pages", []) if isinstance(data, dict) else []
05253:             ranked = []

05200:                         "profile_path": profile_path,

05202:                         "image_key": image_key,
05203:                         "tmdb_id": hit.get("id"),
05204:                         "name": clean_text(hit.get("name", query_name)),
05205:                         "source": "tmdb",
05206:                     })
05207:                     return image_path
05208:     except Exception:
05209:         pass
05210:     return ""

05213: def _wikipedia_actor_category_image(value, settings):
05214:     """Key-free actor portrait fallback using Wikipedia page thumbnails."""
05215:     query_name = _actor_category_clean_name(value)
05216:     wanted = _actor_name_norm(query_name)
05217:     if not wanted:
05218:         return ""
05219:     metadata_lang = clean_text(settings.get("metadata_language", "de-DE")).lower()
05220:     preferred = metadata_lang.split("-")[0] if metadata_lang else "de"
05221:     langs = []
05222:     for lang in (preferred, "de", "en"):
05223:         if lang in ("de", "en", "it", "tr") and lang not in langs:
05224:             langs.append(lang)
05225:     actor_words = (
05226:         "actor", "actress", "schauspieler", "schauspielerin", "darsteller", "darstellerin",
05227:         "attore", "attrice", "oyuncu", "film actor", "television actor"
05228:     )
05229:     headers = {"User-Agent": "EpiMediaHub/%s (actor-category-image)" % PLUGIN_VERSION, "Accept": "application/json"}
05230:     for lang in langs:
05231:         try:
05232:             params = {
05233:                 "action": "query",
05234:                 "generator": "search",
05235:                 "gsrsearch": query_name,
05236:                 "gsrnamespace": "0",
05237:                 "gsrlimit": "6",
05238:                 "prop": "pageimages|pageterms",
05239:                 "piprop": "thumbnail",
05240:                 "pithumbsize": "240",
05241:                 "wbptterms": "description",
05242:                 "format": "json",
05243:                 "formatversion": "2",
05244:             }
05245:             url = "https://%s.wikipedia.org/w/api.php?%s" % (lang, urlencode(params))
05246:             response = urlopen(Request(url, headers=headers), timeout=12)
05247:             raw = response.read()
05248:             response.close()
05249:             if isinstance(raw, bytes):
05250:                 raw = raw.decode("utf-8", "ignore")
05251:             data = json.loads(raw)
05252:             pages = data.get("query", {}).get("pages", []) if isinstance(data, dict) else []
05253:             ranked = []
05254:             for page in pages:
05255:                 if not isinstance(page, dict):
05256:                     continue
05257:                 thumb = page.get("thumbnail", {}) if isinstance(page.get("thumbnail", {}), dict) else {}
05258:                 image_url = clean_text(thumb.get("source", ""))
05259:                 if not image_url:
05260:                     continue
05261:                 title = clean_text(page.get("title", ""))
05262:                 terms = page.get("terms", {}) if isinstance(page.get("terms", {}), dict) else {}
05263:                 descriptions = terms.get("description", [])
05264:                 if isinstance(descriptions, str):
05265:                     descriptions = [descriptions]
05266:                 desc = " ".join(clean_text(x) for x in descriptions).lower()
05267:                 score = _actor_name_match_score(query_name, title)
05268:                 if any(word in desc for word in actor_words):
05269:                     score += 25
05270:                 # For non-explicit plain folders demand a strong name match;
05271:                 # for explicitly labelled actor folders allow a good search hit.
05272:                 ranked.append((score, title, image_url, desc))
05273:             ranked.sort(key=lambda item: item[0], reverse=True)
05274:             minimum = 85 if not _actor_explicit_label(value) else 45
05275:             if ranked and ranked[0][0] >= minimum:
05276:                 score, title, image_url, desc = ranked[0]
05277:                 image_key = hashlib.sha1(image_url.encode("utf-8", "ignore")).hexdigest()
05278:                 image_path = download_image_cached(image_url, ACTOR_CACHE_DIR, key=image_key, timeout=12, max_bytes=3 * 1024 * 1024)
05279:                 if image_path:
05280:                     _save_actor_cache_record(value, {
05281:                         "query": query_name,
05282:                         "name": title,
05283:                         "image_url": image_url,
05284:                         "image_key": image_key,
05285:                         "source": "wikipedia-%s" % lang,
05286:                     })
05287:                     return image_path
05288:         except Exception:
05289:             continue
05290:     return ""

05323: def metadata_cache_key(entry, kind, language):
05324:     raw = "%s|%s|%s" % (kind, clean_media_title(entry.get("title", ""), kind).lower(), language)
05325:     return hashlib.sha1(raw.encode("utf-8", "ignore")).hexdigest()

05327: 
05328: def tmdb_lookup(entry, kind):
05329:     settings = load_global_settings()
05330:     language = settings.get("metadata_language", "de-DE")
05331:     cache = read_json(METADATA_CACHE_FILE, {})
05332:     if not isinstance(cache, dict):
05333:         cache = {}
05334:     key = metadata_cache_key(entry, kind, language)
05335:     cached = cache.get(key)
05336:     if isinstance(cached, dict) and int(time.time()) - int(cached.get("cached_at", 0)) < 30 * 86400:
05337:         return cached
05338:     original_title = entry.get("title", "")
05339:     title = clean_media_title(original_title, kind)
05340:     year = extract_year(original_title)
05341:     media_type = "tv" if kind == "series" else "movie"
05342:     params = {"query": title, "language": language, "include_adult": "false"}
05343:     if year:
05344:         params["first_air_date_year" if media_type == "tv" else "year"] = year
05345:     result = tmdb_request("/search/%s" % media_type, params, settings)
05346:     results = result.get("results", []) if isinstance(result, dict) else []
05347:     if not results:
05348:         raise Exception("Keine passenden TMDb-Daten gefunden.")
05349:     hit = results[0]
05350:     media_id = hit.get("id")
05351:     details = tmdb_request("/%s/%s" % (media_type, media_id), {"language": language}, settings)
05352:     release = details.get("first_air_date", "") if media_type == "tv" else details.get("release_date", "")
05353:     runtime = 0
05354:     if media_type == "tv":
05355:         runtimes = details.get("episode_run_time", []) or []
05356:         runtime = runtimes[0] if runtimes else 0
05357:     else:
05358:         runtime = details.get("runtime", 0) or 0
05359:     metadata = {
05360:         "cached_at": int(time.time()),
05361:         "title": details.get("name") if media_type == "tv" else details.get("title"),
05362:         "original_title": details.get("original_name") if media_type == "tv" else details.get("original_title"),
05363:         "rating": details.get("vote_average", 0),
05364:         "votes": details.get("vote_count", 0),
05365:         "overview": details.get("overview", ""),
05366:         "release": release,
05367:         "runtime": runtime,
05368:         "genres": [g.get("name", "") for g in details.get("genres", []) if isinstance(g, dict)],
05369:         "poster_path": details.get("poster_path", ""),
05370:         "tmdb_id": media_id,
05371:     }
05372:     cache[key] = metadata
05373:     write_json(METADATA_CACHE_FILE, cache)
05374:     return metadata

05377: def tmdb_poster_file(metadata):
05378:     poster_path = clean_text((metadata or {}).get("poster_path", ""))
05379:     if not poster_path:
05380:         return ""
05381:     key = hashlib.sha1(poster_path.encode("utf-8", "ignore")).hexdigest()
05382:     cached = _cached_image_path(POSTER_CACHE_DIR, key)
05383:     if cached:
05384:         return cached
05385:     url = "https://image.tmdb.org/t/p/w500%s" % poster_path
05386:     return download_image_cached(url, POSTER_CACHE_DIR, key=key, timeout=12)

05391: def _weather_condition(code, fallback=""):
05392:     mapping = {
05393:         113: "Klar", 116: "Leicht bewölkt", 119: "Bewölkt", 122: "Bedeckt",
05394:         143: "Nebel", 176: "Regenschauer", 179: "Schneeschauer", 182: "Schneeregen",
05395:         185: "Nieselregen", 200: "Gewitter", 227: "Schnee", 230: "Starker Schnee",
05396:         248: "Nebel", 260: "Gefrierender Nebel", 263: "Nieselregen", 266: "Nieselregen",
05397:         281: "Gefrierender Nieselregen", 284: "Starker Nieselregen", 293: "Leichter Regen",
05398:         296: "Regen", 299: "Regen", 302: "Regen", 305: "Starker Regen", 308: "Starkregen",
05399:         311: "Gefrierender Regen", 314: "Gefrierender Regen", 317: "Schneeregen", 320: "Schneeregen",
05400:         323: "Leichter Schnee", 326: "Schnee", 329: "Schnee", 332: "Schnee",
05401:         335: "Starker Schnee", 338: "Starker Schnee", 350: "Graupel", 353: "Regenschauer",
05402:         356: "Starke Schauer", 359: "Starkregen", 362: "Schneeregenschauer", 365: "Schneeregenschauer",
05403:         368: "Schneeschauer", 371: "Starke Schneeschauer", 374: "Graupelschauer", 377: "Graupelschauer",
05404:         386: "Gewitter", 389: "Starkes Gewitter", 392: "Gewitter mit Schnee", 395: "Starker Schneeschauer"
05405:     }
05406:     try:
05407:         code = int(code)
05408:     except Exception:
05409:         code = 0

05490: def _resolve_weather_query(requested):
05491:     """Return (wttr_query, display_name) for a configured weather location.
05492: 
05493:     A five-digit value is treated as a German postal code. Nominatim is used
05494:     only for this explicit user request and is protected by the normal weather
05495:     cache, so this does not create repeated geocoding traffic. Coordinates are
05496:     then passed to wttr.in for more local weather than a city-name lookup.
05497:     """
05498:     requested = clean_text(requested)
05499:     if not re.match(r"^\d{5}$", requested):
05500:         return requested, requested
05501:     try:
05502:         params = urlencode({
05503:             "postalcode": requested,
05504:             "country": "Germany",
05505:             "countrycodes": "de",
05506:             "format": "jsonv2",
05507:             "addressdetails": "1",
05508:             "limit": "1"
05509:         })
05510:         url = "https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search?%s" % params
05511:         req = Request(url, headers={
05512:             "User-Agent": "EpiMediaHub/%s (weather postal-code lookup)" % PLUGIN_VERSION,
05513:             "Accept": "application/json"
05514:         })
05515:         resp = urlopen(req, timeout=7)
05516:         try:
05517:             raw = resp.read()
05518:         finally:
05519:             try:
05520:                 resp.close()
05521:             except Exception:
05522:                 pass
05523:         if not isinstance(raw, str):
05524:             raw = raw.decode("utf-8", "replace")
05525:         result = json.loads(raw)
05526:         if isinstance(result, list) and result:
05527:             hit = result[0] if isinstance(result[0], dict) else {}
05528:             lat = clean_text(hit.get("lat", ""))
05529:             lon = clean_text(hit.get("lon", ""))
05530:             addr = hit.get("address", {}) if isinstance(hit.get("address", {}), dict) else {}
05531:             district = ""
05532:             for key in ("suburb", "city_district", "borough", "quarter", "neighbourhood"):
05533:                 district = clean_text(addr.get(key, ""))
05534:                 if district:
05535:                     break
05536:             city = ""
05537:             for key in ("city", "town", "village", "municipality", "county"):
05538:                 city = clean_text(addr.get(key, ""))
05539:                 if city:
05540:                     break
05541:             if district and city and district.lower() != city.lower():
05542:                 display = "%s · %s" % (district, city)
05543:             else:
05544:                 display = district or city or requested

05663:     def _startIntroAudio(self):
05664:         if self._intro_audio_started or not os.path.isfile(INTRO_SOUND):

05980:         def applyPreferredAudio(self):
05981:             try:
05982:                 service = self.session.nav.getCurrentService()
05983:                 tracks = service.audioTracks() if service else None
05984:                 if tracks is None:
05985:                     return
05986:                 best_index = -1
05987:                 best_score = 999
05988:                 for index in range(tracks.getNumberOfTracks()):
05989:                     info = tracks.getTrackInfo(index)
05990:                     language = info.getLanguage() if info else ""
05991:                     score = audio_match_score(language, self._epi_preferences)
05992:                     if score < best_score:
05993:                         best_score = score
05994:                         best_index = index
05995:                 if best_index >= 0 and best_score < 999:
05996:                     tracks.selectTrack(best_index)
05997:             except Exception:
05998:                 pass

06307:     def _serviceLooksPlayable(self):
06308:         try:
06309:             service = self.session.nav.getCurrentService()
06310:             if service is None:
06311:                 return False
06312:             info = service.info()
06313:             if info is None or iServiceInformation is None:
06314:                 return self._stream_started
06315:             for attr in ("sVideoWidth", "sVideoHeight", "sAudioPID"):
06316:                 key = getattr(iServiceInformation, attr, None)
06317:                 if key is None:
06318:                     continue
06319:                 try:
06320:                     if int(info.getInfo(key)) > 0:
06321:                         return True
06322:                 except Exception:
06323:                     pass
06324:         except Exception:
06325:             pass
06326:         return self._stream_started

06335:         if self._stream_failure_shown:
06336:             return

06338:         try:
06339:             self._stream_watchdog.stop()

06344:         except Exception:

06348:             self["next"].setText("")
06349:             self.showBanner()
06350:         except Exception:
06351:             pass
06352:         # v0.8.1: make a broken/offline stream impossible to miss. The dialog
06353:         # is intentionally time-limited, so users can dismiss it immediately
06354:         # with OK/EXIT or simply wait a few seconds and continue zapping.

06709:                     widget.hide()
06710:                 except Exception:
06711:                     pass

06719:                     widget.show()

06722:             except Exception:

06724:                     widget.hide()

06728:     def _startHealthJob(self):
06729:         if self._health_job is not None and self._health_job.is_alive():
06730:             return
06731:         queue = [dict(p) for p in self.profiles if clean_text(p.get("m3u_url", "")) or clean_text(p.get("managed_file", ""))]
06732:         if not queue:
06733:             self._updateStatusSlots()
06734:             return
06735:         self._health_stop = False
06736:         self._health_job = threading.Thread(target=self._healthWorker, args=(queue,))
06737:         self._health_job.daemon = True
06738:         self._health_job.start()
06739:         try:
06740:             self._health_timer.start(180, True)
06741:         except TypeError:
06742:             self._health_timer.start(180)

07127:     def _startHomeClock(self):
07128:         self._updateHomeClock()
07129: 
07130:     def _stopHomeClock(self):
07131:         try:
07132:             self._clock_timer.stop()
07133:         except Exception:
07134:             pass
07135: 
07136:     def _updateHomeClock(self):
07137:         try:
07138:             self["clock"].setText(time.strftime("%H:%M"))
07139:         except Exception:
07140:             pass
07141:         try:
07142:             # Update shortly after the next minute starts.
07143:             sec = int(time.localtime().tm_sec)
07144:             delay = max(1000, (61 - sec) * 1000)
07145:             self._clock_timer.start(delay, True)
07146:         except TypeError:
07147:             try:
07148:                 self._clock_timer.start(30000)
07149:             except Exception:
07150:                 pass
07151:         except Exception:
07152:             pass
07153: 
07154:     def requestExit(self):
07155:         self.session.openWithCallback(self._exitConfirmed, EpiExitConfirm)
07156: 
07157:     def _exitConfirmed(self, confirmed=False):
07158:         if confirmed:
07159:             self.close()
07160: 
07161:     def openHelp(self):
07162:         self.session.open(EpiHelpScreen)
07163: 
07164:     def refresh(self):
07165:         profile = get_profile(self.profile_id)
07166:         if profile is None:
07167:             data = load_profiles()
07168:             profiles = data.get("profiles", [])
07169:             if profiles:
07170:                 self.profile_id = profiles[0].get("id")
07171:                 set_active_profile(self.profile_id)
07172:                 profile = profiles[0]
07173: 
07174:         self.tiles = [tr("live"), tr("movies"), tr("series"), tr("search"), tr("switch_playlist"), tr("settings")]
07175:         self["hint"].setText(tr("home_hint"))
07176:         self["subtitle"].setText(
07177:             "%s: %s" % (tr("playlist"), profile.get("name", "Playlist") if profile else tr("no_playlist"))
07178:         )
07179:         self.updateUI()
07180:         self.updateStatus()
07181:         self._paintWeather()
07182: 
07183:         if self.profile_id and playlist_index_needs_rebuild(self.profile_id):
07184:             self._startIndexRebuild()
07185: 
07186:     def _startIndexRebuild(self):
07187:         if self._index_job is not None and self._index_job.is_alive():
07188:             return
07189:         profile_id = self.profile_id
07190:         self._index_result = None
07191:         self["hint"].setText("%s …" % tr("playlist"))
07192:         self._index_job = threading.Thread(target=self._indexWorker, args=(profile_id,))
07193:         self._index_job.daemon = True

07614:     def globalSearchEntered(self, query):
07615:         if not query:
07616:             return
07617:         profile = get_profile(self.profile_id) or {}
07618:         if db_source_mode(self.profile_id) == "xtream":
07619:             try:
07620:                 xtream_load_kind(self.profile_id, "movies")
07621:                 xtream_load_kind(self.profile_id, "series")
07622:             except Exception as error:
07623:                 self.session.open(MessageBox, tr("search_index_error", error=str(error)), MessageBox.TYPE_ERROR, timeout=6)
07624:                 return
07625:         allowed = {
07626:             "movies": set(allowed_category_names(profile, "movies", include_locked=False)),
07627:             "series": set(allowed_category_names(profile, "series", include_locked=False)),
07628:         }
07629:         results = []
07630:         for entry in playlist_entries(self.profile_id, query=query):
07631:             kind = entry.get("type")
07632:             if kind not in ("movies", "series"):
07633:                 continue
07634:             group = clean_text(entry.get("group")) or "Ohne Kategorie"
07635:             if group in allowed.get(kind, set()):
07636:                 results.append(entry)
07637: 
07638:         self.session.open(
07639:             EpiContentScreen,
07640:             self.profile_id,
07641:             tr("search_results", query=query),
07642:             results,
07643:             "mixed",
07644:             allowed
07645:         )

07633:                 continue

07672:         # Do not decode/reload up to ten category images for every key-repeat
07673:         # event. A short one-shot debounce lets native MenuList scrolling stay

07626:             "movies": set(allowed_category_names(profile, "movies", include_locked=False)),
07627:             "series": set(allowed_category_names(profile, "series", include_locked=False)),
07628:         }

07631:             kind = entry.get("type")
07632:             if kind not in ("movies", "series"):
07633:                 continue
07634:             group = clean_text(entry.get("group")) or "Ohne Kategorie"
07635:             if group in allowed.get(kind, set()):
07636:                 results.append(entry)

07639:             EpiContentScreen,

07641:             tr("search_results", query=query),

07644:             allowed

07737:     def _categoryIconPath(self, category_name):
07738:         if self.kind == "live":
07739:             entry = first_cached_live_entry(self.profile_id, category_name)
07740:             if entry:
07741:                 path = picon_local_file(entry)
07742:                 if path:
07743:                     return path

07745: 
07746:         # VOD/series categories usually have no provider image of their own.
07747:         # Recognise common platform names and show a compact local badge.
07748:         # This is deliberately local/offline and does not require a TMDb key.
07749:         name = clean_text(category_name).lower()
07750:         if category_name in ("__all__", "__recent__"):
07751:             return HOME_ICONS[1] if self.kind == "movies" else HOME_ICONS[2]
07752:         rules = (
07753:             (("netflix",), "netflix"),
07754:             (("amazon", "prime video", "primevideo", "prime"), "prime"),
07755:             (("disney+", "disney plus", "disney"), "disney"),
07756:             (("paramount+", "paramount plus", "paramount"), "paramount"),
07757:             (("apple tv+", "apple tv", "appletv"), "apple"),
07758:             (("hbo max", "hbomax", "max"), "max"),
07759:             (("sky",), "sky"),
07760:             (("dazn",), "dazn"),
07761:         )
07762:         for aliases, key in rules:
07763:             if any(alias in name for alias in aliases):
07764:                 path = CATEGORY_SERVICE_ICONS.get(key, "")
07765:                 if path and os.path.isfile(path):
07766:                     return path

07773:                 return saga_path

07882:     def _restartLoadTimer(self):
07883:         try:
07884:             self._load_timer.start(250, True)
07885:         except TypeError:
07886:             self._load_timer.start(250)
07887: 
07888:     def _startXtreamLoad(self, mode, name="", allowed=None, query=""):
07889:         if self._load_job is not None and self._load_job.is_alive():
07890:             self["info"].setText(tr("loading_busy"))
07891:             return
07892:         self._pending_load = {
07893:             "mode": mode,
07894:             "name": name,
07895:             "allowed": list(allowed or []),
07896:             "query": query,
07897:         }
07898:         self._load_result = None
07899:         if mode == "category":
07900:             self["info"].setText(tr("loading_category", name=name))
07901:         elif mode == "all":
07902:             self["info"].setText(tr("loading_all", kind=type_label(self.kind)))
07903:         else:
07904:             self["info"].setText(tr("loading_search", kind=type_label(self.kind)))
07905:         self._load_job = threading.Thread(
07906:             target=self._xtreamLoadWorker,
07907:             args=(self.profile_id, self.kind, mode, name)
07908:         )
07909:         self._load_job.daemon = True
07910:         self._load_job.start()
07911:         self._restartLoadTimer()
07912: 
07913:     def _xtreamLoadWorker(self, profile_id, kind, mode, name):
07914:         try:
07915:             if mode == "category":
07916:                 entries, cat_order = xtream_fetch_category_entries(profile_id, kind, name)
07917:                 self._load_result = (True, len(entries), "", entries, cat_order)
07918:             else:
07919:                 count = xtream_load_kind(profile_id, kind)
07920:                 self._load_result = (True, count, "", None, 0)
07921:         except Exception as error:
07922:             self._load_result = (False, 0, str(error), None, 0)
07923: 
07924:     def _pollXtreamLoad(self):
07925:         if self._load_job is not None and self._load_job.is_alive():
07926:             self._restartLoadTimer()
07927:             return
07928:         result = self._load_result
07929:         pending = self._pending_load or {}
07930:         self._load_job = None
07931:         self._load_result = None
07932:         self._pending_load = None
07933:         if not result:
07934:             return
07935:         if not result[0]:
07936:             self["info"].setText("Laden fehlgeschlagen")
07937:             self.session.open(
07938:                 MessageBox,
07939:                 tr("content_load_error", error=result[2]),
07940:                 MessageBox.TYPE_ERROR,
07941:                 timeout=7
07942:             )
07943:             return
07944: 
07945:         self.refreshRows()
07946:         mode = pending.get("mode")
07947:         allowed = pending.get("allowed") or allowed_category_names(
07948:             get_profile(self.profile_id) or {}, self.kind, include_locked=False
07949:         )
07950:         if mode == "category":
07951:             name = pending.get("name", "")
07952:             items = result[3] if len(result) > 3 and isinstance(result[3], list) else []
07953:             cat_order = result[4] if len(result) > 4 else 0
07954:             if not items:
07955:                 items = playlist_entries(self.profile_id, kind=self.kind, group=name)
07956:             else:
07957:                 cache_thread = threading.Thread(
07958:                     target=xtream_cache_category_entries,
07959:                     args=(self.profile_id, self.kind, name, list(items), cat_order)
07960:                 )
07961:                 cache_thread.daemon = True
07962:                 cache_thread.start()
07963:             self._openItems(name, items, allowed)
07964:         elif mode == "all":
07965:             items = playlist_entries(self.profile_id, kind=self.kind, allowed_groups=allowed)
07966:             self._openItems("%s %s" % (tr("all"), type_label(self.kind)), items, allowed)
07967:         elif mode == "search":
07968:             query = pending.get("query", "")
07969:             items = playlist_entries(
07970:                 self.profile_id,
07971:                 kind=self.kind,
07972:                 query=query,
07973:                 allowed_groups=allowed
07974:             )
07975:             self._openItems(tr("search_results", query=query), items, allowed)
07976:

07977:     def refreshRows(self):
07978:         self.profile = get_profile(self.profile_id) or self.profile
07979:         hidden = hidden_category_set(self.profile, self.kind)
07980:         locked = effective_locked_category_set(self.profile, self.kind)
07981: 
07982:         records = []
07983:         self.category_counts = {}
07984:         for name, count in playlist_categories(self.profile_id, self.kind):
07985:             self.category_counts[name] = count
07986:             if name in hidden:
07987:                 continue
07988:             records.append((name, count))
07989: 
07990:         self.original_categories = list(records)
07991: 
07992:         if self.sort_mode == 1:
07993:             records = sorted(records, key=lambda item: item[0].lower())
07994:         elif self.sort_mode == 2:
07995:             records = sorted(records, key=lambda item: item[0].lower(), reverse=True)
07996: 
07997:         pseudo = ["__all__"]
07998:         if self.kind in ("live", "movies", "series"):
07999:             pseudo = ["__recent__", "__all__"]
08000:         self.category_names = pseudo + [name for name, _count in records]
08001: 
08002:         allowed_for_all = [
08003:             name for name, _count in records if name not in locked
08004:         ]
08005:         known_counts = [self.category_counts.get(name, -1) for name in allowed_for_all]
08006:         all_known = all(value >= 0 for value in known_counts)
08007:         all_count = sum(value for value in known_counts if value >= 0)
08008: 
08009:         # All three media category screens reserve room for the detached icon strip.
08010:         indent = "             " if self.kind in ("live", "movies", "series") else ""
08011:         rows = []
08012:         if self.kind in ("live", "movies", "series"):
08013:             recent_count = len(visible_recent_entries(self.profile_id, self.kind, self.profile, 40))
08014:             rows.append("%s%s   (%d)" % (indent, tr("recent").upper(), recent_count))
08015:         rows.append("%s%s   (%s)" % (indent, tr("all"), str(all_count) if all_known else "…"))
08016:         for name, count in records:
08017:             marker = " [PIN]" if name in locked else ""
08018:             count_text = str(count) if count >= 0 else "…"
08019:             rows.append("%s%s%s   (%s)" % (indent, name, marker, count_text))
08020:         self["list"].setList(rows)
08021:         self._scheduleCategoryPiconRefresh(60)
08022:         self["info"].setText(tr("category_info", count=len(records)))
08023:

08027:             self.session.openWithCallback(
08028:                 self._managePinEntered,
08029:                 EpiPinScreen,
08030:                 "Kategorien verwalten - Kindersicherungs-PIN"
08031:             )
08032:             return
08033:         self._openCategoryManager()

08325:     def openSeason(self):
08326:         try:
08327:             index = self["list"].getSelectedIndex()
08328:         except Exception:
08329:             index = -1
08330:         if index < 0 or index >= len(self.season_groups):
08331:             return
08332:         group = self.season_groups[index]
08333:         episodes = list(group.get("episodes", []))
08334:         if not episodes:
08335:             self.session.open(MessageBox, tr("season_empty"), MessageBox.TYPE_INFO, timeout=3)
08336:             return
08337:         title = "%s - %s" % (self.series_title, clean_text(group.get("label", "Staffel")))
08338:         self.session.open(
08339:             EpiContentScreen,
08340:             self.profile_id,
08341:             title,
08342:             episodes,
08343:             "series",
08344:             [self.series_title]
08345:         )

08348: class EpiContentScreen(Screen):
08349:     skin = CONTENT_SKIN
08350: 
08351:     def __init__(self, session, profile_id, title, visible_items, kind, search_allowed_groups=None):
08352:         Screen.__init__(self, session)
08353:         self.profile_id = profile_id
08354:         self.kind = kind
08355:         self.visible_items = list(visible_items or [])
08356:         self.original_items = list(self.visible_items)
08357:         self.search_allowed_groups = search_allowed_groups
08358:         self._is_recent_view = clean_text(title) == clean_text(tr("recent"))
08359:         self.sort_mode = 0
08360: 
08361:         self["logo"] = Pixmap()
08362:         self["title"] = Label(title)

08465:     def _updateLivePiconStrip(self):
08466:         """Render cached Picons beside the normal MenuList without replacing it."""
08467:         if self.kind != "live":
08468:             return
08469:         try:

08472:                 top = 0
08473:         except Exception:
08474:             try:
08475:                 current = self["list"].getSelectedIndex()
08476:             except Exception:
08477:                 current = 0
08478:             top = max(0, current - 4)
08479:         for slot, name in enumerate(getattr(self, "_live_picon_slots", [])):
08480:             idx = top + slot
08481:             try:
08482:                 widget = self[name]
08483:                 if idx >= len(self.visible_items):

08475:                 current = self["list"].getSelectedIndex()
08476:             except Exception:
08477:                 current = 0
08478:             top = max(0, current - 4)
08479:         for slot, name in enumerate(getattr(self, "_live_picon_slots", [])):
08480:             idx = top + slot
08481:             try:
08482:                 widget = self[name]
08483:                 if idx >= len(self.visible_items):
08484:                     widget.hide()
08485:                     continue
08486:                 path = picon_local_file(self.visible_items[idx])
08487:                 if path and os.path.isfile(path) and widget.instance is not None:
08488:                     try:
08489:                         widget.instance.setPixmapFromFile(path)
08490:                         widget.show()
08491:                     except Exception:
08492:                         widget.hide()
08493:                 else:
08494:                     widget.hide()
08495:             except Exception:
08496:                 pass

08512:     def _liveRowEpgText(self, entry):
08513:         current, next_item = epg_now_next_quick(self.profile_id, entry)
08514:         if current:
08515:             return "JETZT %s-%s  %s" % (format_clock(current.get("start")), format_clock(current.get("stop")), clean_text(current.get("title", "")))
08516:         if next_item:
08517:             return "AB %s  %s" % (format_clock(next_item.get("start")), clean_text(next_item.get("title", "")))
08518:         return "EPG wird automatisch geladen …"

08520:     def refreshRows(self):
08521:         try:
08522:             selected = self["list"].getSelectedIndex()
08523:         except Exception:
08524:             selected = 0
08525:         favs = favorite_keys(self.profile_id)
08526:         rows = []
08527:         detected_kind = ""
08528:         if self.visible_items:
08529:             detected_kind = self.visible_items[0].get("type", "")
08530:         for entry in self.visible_items:
08531:             prefix = "* " if entry.get("url", "") in favs else ""
08532:             title = prefix + (entry.get("recent_title") or entry.get("title", "Unbenannt"))
08533:             if detected_kind == "live":
08534:                 epg_text = self._liveRowEpgText(entry)
08535:                 # Keep the normal string list so OpenATV owns navigation again.
08536:                 # Leading space leaves room for the independent Picon strip.
08537:                 rows.append("             %s   |   %s" % (title, epg_text))
08538:             else:
08539:                 rows.append(title)
08540:         self["list"].setList(rows)
08541:         if rows:
08542:             try:
08543:                 self["list"].moveToIndex(max(0, min(selected, len(rows) - 1)))
08544:             except Exception:
08545:                 pass
08546:         if detected_kind == "live":
08547:             self._updateLivePiconStrip()
08548:         extra = "INFO/0 = EPG" if detected_kind == "live" else "INFO/0 = %s" % tr("movie_info")
08549:         if detected_kind == "live":
08550:             extra += "   |   EPG + Picons automatisch"
08551:         self["info"].setText("%d %s   |   * = %s   |   %s" % (len(self.visible_items), tr("entries"), tr("favorite"), extra))
08552: 
08553:     def _kickLivePreview(self):
08554:         if self.kind != "live" or not self.visible_items:
08555:             return
08556:         try:
08557:             index = self["list"].getSelectedIndex()
08558:         except Exception:
08559:             index = 0
08560:         start = max(0, index - 4)
08561:         end = min(len(self.visible_items), index + 14)
08562:         window = (start, end)
08563:         if self._live_preview_job is not None and self._live_preview_job.is_alive():
08564:             self._scheduleLivePreview(350)
08565:             return
08566:         # Revisit the same window periodically, because the automatic XMLTV
08567:         # cache may have finished in the meantime even if short EPG was absent.
08568:         self._live_preview_window = window
08569:         entries = [dict(item) for item in self.visible_items[start:end]]
08570:         self._live_preview_result = None
08571:         self._live_preview_job = threading.Thread(target=self._livePreviewWorker, args=(entries,))
08572:         self._live_preview_job.daemon = True
08573:         self._live_preview_job.start()
08574:         self._scheduleLivePreview(250)
08575: 
08576:     def _scheduleLivePreview(self, delay=700):
08577:         try:
08578:             self._live_preview_timer.start(int(delay), True)
08579:         except TypeError:
08580:             self._live_preview_timer.start(int(delay))
08581: 
08582:     def _livePreviewWorker(self, entries):
08583:         meta = load_xtream_meta(self.profile_id)
08584:         use_xtream = meta.get("source") == "xtream"
08585:         def one(entry):
08586:             # Picon and per-channel short EPG are deliberately lazy: only the
08587:             # currently visible window is touched.
08588:             ensure_picon_file(entry)
08589:             if use_xtream and not epg_now_next_quick(self.profile_id, entry)[0]:
08590:                 try:

===== lines 8747-8862 =====
08747:                 metadata = entry_basic_metadata(entry)
08748:             else:
08749:                 metadata = best_metadata_lookup(self.profile_id, entry, kind)
08750:             poster = metadata_poster_file(metadata)
08751:             self._media_preview_result = (token, True, metadata, poster, "")
08752:         except Exception as error:
08753:             self._media_preview_result = (token, False, entry_basic_metadata(entry), "", str(error))

08755:     def _pollMediaPreview(self):

08820:     def sortItems(self):
08821:         self.sort_mode = (self.sort_mode + 1) % 3
08822:         if self.sort_mode == 0:
08823:             self.visible_items = list(self.original_items)

08843:             return

09013:     def vodPlayerClosed(self, *args):
09014:         # If playback was launched from "Recently watched", reload that view so
09015:         # the episode shown there immediately reflects the latest auto-advanced
09016:         # or completed episode.
09017:         if self._is_recent_view and self.kind in ("movies", "series"):

09020:                 self.visible_items = visible_recent_entries(self.profile_id, self.kind, profile, 40)
09021:                 self.original_items = list(self.visible_items)
09022:             except Exception:
09023:                 pass
09024:         self.refreshRows()
09025: 
09026:     def livePlayerClosed(self, current_url=""):
09027:         if not current_url:
09028:             return
09029:         for index, item in enumerate(self.visible_items):
09030:             if item.get("url", "") == current_url:
09031:                 try:
09032:                     self["list"].moveToIndex(index)
09033:                 except Exception:
09034:                     pass
09035:                 break

09624:     def _currentPinEntered(self, pin):
09625:         action = self._pin_action
09626:         if pin is None:
09627:             self._pin_action = ""
09628:             return
09629:         if not pin_matches(pin):
09630:             self._pin_action = ""
09631:             self.session.open(MessageBox, "PIN ist falsch.", MessageBox.TYPE_ERROR, timeout=3)
09632:             return
09633: 
09634:         self.settings = load_global_settings()
09635:         if action == "disable":
09636:             self.settings["parental_enabled"] = False
09637:             save_global_settings(self.settings)
09638:             self._pin_action = ""
09639:             self.refresh()
09640:         elif action == "adultauto":
09641:             self.settings["parental_auto_adult"] = not self.settings.get("parental_auto_adult", True)
09642:             save_global_settings(self.settings)
09643:             self._pin_action = ""
09644:             self.refresh()
09645:         elif action == "change_pin_auth":
09646:             self._pin_action = "change_pin"
09647:             self._startNewPin()

09649:     def _retryFastApiConfirmed(self, confirmed):
09650:         if confirmed:
09651:             # Reuses the existing background playlist worker. In 0.7.1 it tries
09652:             # direct URL, redirects and cached M3U stream credentials before any

09948: def route_after_splash(session):
09949:     scan_local_playlists()
09950:     data = load_profiles()
09951:     profiles = data.get("profiles", [])
09952:     if len(profiles) == 1:
09953:         profile_id = profiles[0].get("id")
09954:         set_active_profile(profile_id)
09955:         session.open(EpiMediaHubHome, profile_id)
09956:     elif len(profiles) > 1:
09957:         session.openWithCallback(lambda profile_id=None: open_home_after_selection(session, profile_id), EpiProfileSelect)
09958:     else:
09959:         session.openWithCallback(lambda profile_id=None: open_home_after_selection(session, profile_id), EpiProfileSelect)
09960: 
09961: 
09962: def open_home_after_selection(session, profile_id):
09963:     if profile_id:
09964:         set_active_profile(profile_id)
09965:         session.open(EpiMediaHubHome, profile_id)

09968: def main(session, **kwargs):
09969:     ensure_data_dir()
09970:     session.openWithCallback(lambda *args: route_after_splash(session), EpiSplash)

09972: 
09973: # Keep timers/session alive globally for boot and standby-wakeup launches.
09974: # Opening a full-screen plugin directly inside WHERE_SESSIONSTART or while
09975: # Enigma2 is still closing its Standby screen can be too early on some images,
